Fusion further includes lightweight routing modules that adapt compression strength to each input and support inference-time adjustment of the accuracy--latency trade-off without retraining. On ImageNet-1k with DeiT-S, Fusion matches or surpasses state-of-the-art adaptive ViT methods at comparable compute budgets while reducing calibration error by up to $4\times$ and inference energy by $48\%$. Experiments across ImageNet-100, CIFAR-100, and ImageNette with multiple ViT backbones demonstrate consistent transferability without dataset-specific tuning.

</details>


### [35] [SE-UNet: Singular Equivariant Imaging for Real-World Constrained Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02628)
*Kanishk Awadhiya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SE-UNet提出了一种基于几何等变性和奇异值门控的框架，用于解决病态成像问题，无需大量预训练数据，在CIFAR-10上实现了先进的零样本修复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然扩散模型在图像合成领域取得了革命性进展，但在现实世界逆问题应用中面临两大挑战：1）需要大量训练数据集；2）难以施加严格的物理约束。这些问题限制了扩散模型在受限生成任务中的实际应用。

Method: SE-UNet（奇异等变UNet）框架将生成问题视为受几何等变性和奇异值门控约束的优化问题。具体方法包括：1）利用D4群几何等变性约束；2）引入奇异值门控机制；3）通过这些强归纳偏置标准化解空间。

Result: 在CIFAR-10数据集上，SE-UNet在80%像素缺失的零样本修复任务中取得了最先进结果。相比Deep Image Prior基线，PSNR提升超过4dB。模型表现出独特的"奇异捕捉"收敛特性——能够快速锁定到信号流形。

Conclusion: SE-UNet为受限生成任务提供了一条数据高效的途径，通过结合理论先验与实用部署需求，实现了ReALM-GEN目标中理论先验与实际部署的桥梁作用。

Abstract: While diffusion models have revolutionized image synthesis, their application to real-world inverse problems is often hampered by the need for massive datasets and the difficulty of imposing strict physical constraints. In this work, we introduce \textbf{SE-UNet} (Singular Equivariant UNet), a framework designed to solve ill-posed imaging tasks without extensive pre-training. By treating generation as an optimization problem constrained by geometric equivariance ($D_4$ group) and singular value gating, SE-UNet effectively standardizes the solution space. We demonstrate that these strong inductive biases allow for state-of-the-art zero-shot inpainting results (80\% missing pixels) on CIFAR-10. Our method surpasses Deep Image Prior (DIP) baselines by over 4 dB in PSNR and exhibits a characteristic "singular snap" convergence -- rapidly locking into the signal manifold. SE-UNet thus offers a data-efficient pathway for constrained generation, aligning with the ReALM-GEN goal of bridging theoretical priors with practical deployment.

</details>


### [36] [CLABTOOLKIT: An Open-Source Toolkit for Routine Processing, Manipulation, and Visualization of Neuroimaging Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02638)
*Yasser Alemán-Gómez,Nino Hervé,Patric Hagmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLABTOOLKIT是一个开源的Python工具包，它将神经影像学中异构数据（体积MRI、表面网格、纤维束等）的操作统一到单个框架中，通过五种核心数据结构实现跨格式的数据处理和可视化，解决了不同工具接口不兼容的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经影像学研究需要处理多种异构数据结构（原始MRI体积数据、体积分割、皮质表面网格、纤维束成像和连接矩阵），这些数据在不同工具之间具有不兼容的接口和文件格式。研究人员不得不重复实现常规但技术复杂的数据操作，导致效率低下和工作流程碎片化。

Method: 开发了一个开源的Python包，引入五种核心数据结构：Parcellation（分割）、Surface（表面）、AnnotParcellation（注释分割）、Tractogram（纤维束）和Connectome（连接组）。这些对象封装了常见的神经解剖实体，提供跨标准格式（NIfTI、GIFTI、FreeSurfer注释、TCK/TRK等）的统一方法。工具包采用模块化设计，包含19个模块、13个面向对象类、234个方法和207个独立函数，支持BIDS数据集管理、FreeSurfer集成、扩散MRI处理、形态学分析、图论网络分析和GPU加速可视化。

Result: 成功创建了一个完整的神经影像学数据处理框架，能够处理从分割和表面数据到纤维束和连接组生成的完整工作流。工具包支持Python 3.9-3.12，采用Apache 2.0许可证发布，提供了源代码、文档和示例工作流程。与现有库不同，它将颜色查找表管理、分割操作、多表面可视化和纤维束成像实用工具整合在单一框架中。

Conclusion: CLABTOOLKIT通过统一的数据结构和一致的接口，显著简化了神经影像学数据处理流程，解决了不同工具间的互操作性问题。它的模块化设计和配置系统使得工作流程可定制化，有望提高研究效率并促进可重复性研究。

Abstract: Neuroimaging research requires manipulating heterogeneous data structures, including raw MRI volumes, volumetric parcellations, cortical surface meshes, tractograms, and connectivity matrices, across tools with incompatible interfaces and file formats, forcing researchers to repeatedly re-implement routine but technically demanding operations. We present CLABTOOLKIT, an open-source Python package that consolidates these operations into a single, coherent framework by representing volumetric, surface, and streamline data as interoperable Python objects. Five core data structures (Parcellation, Surface, AnnotParcellation, Tractogram, and Connectome) encapsulate common neuroanatomical entities and provide consistent methods for loading, processing, and exporting data across standard neuroimaging formats (e.g., NIfTI, GIFTI, FreeSurfer annotations, TCK/TRK), including connectome generation from a parcellation and scalar-map projection onto tractogram streamlines. Complementary modules support BIDS dataset management, FreeSurfer integration, diffusion MRI processing, morphometric analysis, graph-theoretical network analysis, and GPU-accelerated multi-panel visualization via PyVista. The toolkit comprises 19 modules organised into six layers, exposing 13 object-oriented classes with 234 methods and 207 standalone functions, and a JSON-based configuration system enables workflow customization without code changes. Unlike existing neuroimaging libraries, which typically address these tasks separately, CLABTOOLKIT combines color and lookup-table management, parcellation manipulation, multi-surface visualization, and tractography utilities within a single framework. CLABTOOLKIT is compatible with Python 3.9-3.12 and released under the Apache 2.0 license. Source code, documentation, and example workflows are available at https://github.com/connectomicslab/clabtoolkit.

</details>


### [37] [BiSLW: Bi-Spectral Latent Watermarking for Generative Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02643)
*Aryan Pandit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BiSLW：一种基于扩散模型的可训练双谱潜在水印框架，通过在解码扩散潜在空间的互补谱带中嵌入对齐的身份信号，提高水印的鲁棒性和视觉保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散生成模型存在未授权使用风险且缺乏可靠溯源方法。现有水印技术通常将潜在张量视为静态空间特征图或依赖像素域修改，未能充分利用潜在空间内部频率结构进行双波段冗余嵌入，容易受到扩散过程的随机性和再生攻击的影响。

Method: 利用扩散潜在固有的频率结构设计双波段水印框架：低频分量编码全局语义，高频分量捕获精细纹理。通过学习的编码器在两个互补谱带中独立注入水印，在解码前重新组合使其成为生成轨迹的内在部分。双谱解码器从各波段恢复水印，同时跨波段一致性约束确保语义和纹理嵌入的对齐。

Result: 实验表明BiSLW在感知保真度和鲁棒性之间取得良好平衡：与先前潜在扩散水印方法相比，PSNR提高超过3dB；在激进的再生攻击和常见失真下保持近乎完美的比特精度；计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: BiSLW通过利用扩散潜在空间的频率结构实现双谱水印嵌入，提供了一种既保持高视觉质量又具有强大鲁棒性的水印解决方案，有效解决了扩散模型的内容溯源和版权保护问题。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative models have transformed visual content synthesis, yet they remain vulnerable to unauthorized usage and lack reliable attribution methods. Existing watermarking techniques often treat latent tensors as static spatial feature maps or depend on pixel-domain modification, and most do not explicitly leverage the internal frequency structure of the latent space for dual-band redundant embedding, leaving them susceptible to the stochastic nature of diffusion and regeneration attacks. We introduce BiSLW, a trainable bi-spectral latent watermarking framework that jointly embeds aligned identity signals across complementary spectral bands of the decoded diffusion latent using learned encoders and decoders, going beyond fixed-pattern frequency approaches. We leverage the inherent frequency structure of diffusion latents to design a dual-band watermarking framework. Low-frequency components encode global semantics, while high-frequency components capture fine texture. We exploit this structure to embed watermarks across complementary spectral bands. The watermark is independently injected into both bands via learned encoders and recombined before decoding, ensuring it becomes intrinsic to the generative trajectory. Dual spectral decoders recover the watermark from each band, while a cross-band consistency constraint enforces alignment between semantic and textural embeddings. Experiments show that BiSLW achieves a strong balance between perceptual fidelity and robustness, improving PSNR by over 3 dB compared to prior latent diffusion watermarking methods while preserving near-perfect bit accuracy under aggressive regeneration and common distortions, all with negligible computational overhead.

</details>


### [38] [EmoteGPT: 3D Human Facial Expressions from Natural Language Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02674)
*Haoran Wang,Mohit Mendiratta,Christian Theobalt,Adam Kortylewski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Txt2Emote：一个包含细粒度文本标注的3D面部表情基准数据集，以及EmoteGPT：基于多模态大语言模型的文本到3D表情生成框架，在3DMM参数空间中进行表情回归，超越了现有文本到3D面部合成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到3D方法联合生成身份、表情和纹理，难以实现细粒度的表情控制。需要将文本驱动的表情合成重新构建为在3DMM解耦参数空间中的回归问题，但这需要连接详细语言描述和精确表情参数的配对数据，而现有资源中缺乏此类数据。

Method: 1) 引入Txt2Emote基准数据集，使用GPT-4o和高保真面部跟踪器获得细粒度文本标注，包含面部特征的显式描述和表情背后情境的隐式描述。2) 提出EmoteGPT框架，基于多模态大语言模型，使用专用<Expr>令牌将表情表示语义接地，然后解码为3DMM参数。3) 通过大规模图像到3DMM数据进行训练增强，提升性能。

Result: EmoteGPT在情感识别指标和感知表现力方面超越了最先进的文本到3D面部合成方法。集成到虚拟形象流程中，该方法能够从文本输入生成逼真和风格化的3D虚拟形象，以及具有3D一致性的2D面部合成。

Conclusion: 该方法通过构建高质量的数据集和基于多模态大语言模型的回归框架，实现了对3D面部表情的精确文本控制，解决了现有方法中难以实现细粒度表情控制的问题，为虚拟形象、动画和人机交互应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Precise control of 3D facial expressions from text is crucial for virtual avatars, animation, and human-computer interaction, yet existing text-to-3D methods jointly generate identity, expression, and texture, making fine-grained expression control difficult. We instead formulate text-driven expression synthesis as a regression problem in the disentangled parameter space of a 3D Morphable Model (3DMM). This setting, however, requires paired data linking detailed language to precise expression parameters, which are missing from existing resources. To fill this gap, we introduce Txt2Emote, a benchmark of diverse 3D facial expressions with fine-grained textual annotations obtained from GPT-4o and a high-fidelity face tracker, providing both explicit descriptions detailing facial features and implicit descriptions referencing the situational context behind the expression. Leveraging this dataset, we present EmoteGPT, a text-to-3D expression framework based on a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) with a dedicated <Expr> token to semantically ground expression representations, which are then decoded into 3DMM parameters. We further improve EmoteGPT by augmenting training with large-scale image-to-3DMM data, enabling it to surpass state-of-the-art text-to-3D face synthesis methods on emotion recognition metrics and in perceived expressiveness. Integrated into avatar pipelines, our method enables photorealistic and stylized 3D avatars, as well as expressive 3D-consistent 2D face synthesis from textual input.

</details>


### [39] [K9-Bench: Evaluating Multimodal LLMs on Canine-Centric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02680)
*Khush Attarde,Yusuf Ali,Megha Thukral,Divye Bhutani,Thomas Ploetz,Zsolt Kira*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出K9-Bench基准测试，专注于真实世界家犬视频的多模态理解，包含5000个问答对，测试MLLMs在犬类动作和交互理解方面的长时序推理能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs在跨模态理解方面表现出色，但在动物中心场景的理解和建模方面研究不足。作为数百万家庭的重要组成部分，AI系统需要能够处理宠物相关任务，如识别痛苦信号、实现响应式机器人伴侣等

Method: 提出可扩展的VLM/LLM驱动数据生成流水线，自动从网络挖掘犬类中心视频，并策划需要细粒度、多跳推理的问答对。实施偏差缓解策略消除VLM在数据集策划中引入的偏差

Result: 前沿MLLMs在犬类中心任务上表现有限的零样本能力：顶级闭源模型优于开源模型，但在理解长时序中细微姿态和交互线索的组合推理方面仍有困难。通用思维链提示对长时序推理效果有限

Conclusion: K9-Bench不仅是犬类活动分析的新数据集，还提供了通用数据集构建流水线，可适应其他低数据领域进行定量分析。该项目网站已公开

Abstract: MLLMs have shown strong zero-shot capabilities across diverse inputs such as across images, video, audio, and text. A crucial, yet underexplored, application of these models lies in understanding and modeling animal-centric scenarios. As animals are integral to millions of households, benchmarking next-generation AI models on pet-focused tasks, ranging from recognizing distress signals to enabling responsive robotic companions, is essential for building AI systems that can work alongside us. We introduce K9-Bench, a novel benchmark focused on real-world domestic dog videos, specifically targeting canine action and interaction understanding via approximately 5000 question-answer pairs across 907 videos spanning 5 distinct task categories that test long-form, canine-centric multimodal reasoning in MLLMs. To create this dataset, we propose a scalable, VLM/LLM-powered data generation pipeline that automatically mines canine-centric videos from the web and curates QA pairs requiring fine-grained, multi-hop reasoning over canine actions and temporally extended interaction sequences. We implement bias mitigation strategies designed to eliminate biases introduced by VLMs during dataset curation. Through extensive experimentation, we find that frontier MLLMs exhibit limited zero-shot performance on canine-centric tasks: although state-of-the-art closed-source models outperform open-source counterparts, they still struggle with compositional reasoning over subtle posture and interaction cues spread over long horizons. We observe that generic chain-of-thought prompting provides only modest performance for such long-horizon reasoning. Beyond a novel dataset for canine activity analysis, K9-Bench provides a general-purpose dataset construction pipeline that can be adapted to other low-data domains for quantitative analysis. Our project website is available at: https://ogmenrobotics.github.io/K9Bench.

</details>


### [40] [S-EMBER: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Streaming Egocentric Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02689)
*Xiaodong Wang,Xuanyi Zhao,Pedro Rodriguez,Devendra Singh Sachan,Barlas Oguz,Seungwhan Moon,Shang-Wen Li,Gargi Ghosh,Xin Dong,Wen-Tau Yih*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: S-EMBER是一个用于流式自我中心记忆检索的新基准测试，包含3,141个视频（388小时），通过Ray-Ban Meta智能眼镜采集，模拟可穿戴设备的实时流式推理场景，揭示了语义推理与时间定位精度之间的瓶颈矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可穿戴设备支持连续的第一人称记录，AI助手需要长时间跨度的推理来回忆过去经历（情景记忆）。当前基准测试通常依赖离线评估，无法模拟可穿戴智能设备的流式现实场景。

Method: 提出S-EMBER（Streaming Egocentric Memory Benchmark for Episodic Retrieval），包含3,141个总计388小时的有机活动视频，通过Ray-Ban Meta智能眼镜采集。基准测试形式化了基于流的情景记忆检索范式，从全局离线搜索转向由连续流中视觉事件触发的因果、主动回忆。提供9,448个需要手动视觉验证的QA对，要求精确的时间定位并支持灵活响应长度。

Result: 通过对前沿模型的广泛基准测试，发现了一个定位悖论：虽然语义推理能力随着参数规模增加而提升，但时间定位精度仍然是一个停滞的架构瓶颈，无法通过粗暴增加模型大小、分辨率或帧密度来改善。

Conclusion: S-EMBER为下一代可穿戴AI代理开发基于真实硬件环境的、可靠的情景记忆系统奠定了硬件真实的基准基础。

Abstract: As wearable devices enable continuous first-person recording, AI assistants must reason across long time horizons to recall past experiences-a capability known as episodic memory. Current benchmarks often rely on offline evaluation with access to entire video files, failing to simulate the streaming reality of wearable intelligence. We introduce S-EMBER (Streaming Egocentric Memory Benchmark for Episodic Retrieval), a large-scale benchmark comprising 3,141 videos totaling 388 hours of organic activity captured via Ray-Ban Meta smart glasses. S-EMBER formalizes grounded streaming episodic retrieval, a paradigm shift from global offline search to causal, active recall triggered by visual events in a continuous stream. We provide 9,448 QA pairs requiring manual visual proof through precise temporal localization and supporting flexible response lengths to simulate natural human-AI interaction. Our extensive benchmarking of frontier models uncovers a localization paradox: while semantic reasoning improves with parameter scale, temporal grounding precision remains a stagnant architectural bottleneck that does not benefit from brute-force increases in model size, resolution, or frame density. S-EMBER establishes a hardware-authentic foundation for developing grounded, reliable episodic memory in the next generation of wearable AI agents.

</details>


### [41] [An Automated Multimodal Glaucoma Detection Framework Using ViT and a Stacking-Based Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02692)
*Ishrat Jahan,Muhammad E. H Chowdhury,Murugappan Murugappan,Kanchon Kanti Podder,Tawsifur Rahman,Shrestha Datta,Md Sakib Abrar Hossain,Md Mosarrof Hossen,Yosra Magdi Salih Mekki,Sanjiban Sekhar Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个自动化多模态框架，结合眼底图像和临床数据进行青光眼检测。在样本层面和患者层面两种评估设置下，使用Vision Transformer提取视觉特征并与经典机器学习模型结合，通过集成学习方法在PAPILA数据集上获得高精度性能，并部署为端到端网络平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 青光眼是一种进展性眼病，早期发现至关重要。传统诊断方法耗时且依赖专家解读，难以实现大规模筛查。因此需要开发自动化、可扩展的诊断框架来克服这些限制。

Method: 1. 采用两种评估设置：样本层面（独立分析单个样本）和患者层面（聚合每位患者数据进行预测）
2. 提出多模态框架，整合眼底图像和临床数据
3. 使用Vision Transformer (ViT)架构提取深度视觉特征
4. 结合经典机器学习模型进行分类
5. 采用基于堆叠的集成学习方法，组合三个最佳性能分类器优化性能

Result: 在PAPILA公开数据集上取得优异诊断性能：
- 样本层面多模态分类：97.47%准确率和97.50% F1分数
- 患者层面检测：98.97%准确率和F1分数
- 进一步将框架部署为端到端网络平台，支持自动化青光眼筛查和临床决策

Conclusion: 本研究提出的自动化多模态框架在青光眼检测方面表现出色，通过整合眼底图像和临床数据，在两个不同评估设置下均达到高性能。该框架已成功部署为网络平台，为大规模青光眼筛查和临床决策支持提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Glaucoma is a progressive eye disease that can lead to irreversible vision loss if not detected at an early stage. Conventional diagnostic procedures are often time-consuming and rely heavily on expert interpretation, limiting their scalability for large-scale screening. In this study, glaucoma detection is investigated under two evaluation settings: sample-wise, where individual samples are analyzed independently, and patient-wise, where data from each patient are aggregated for final prediction. An automated multimodal framework is proposed that integrates fundus images with clinical data. Under the sample-wise setting, detection is performed using fundus images and clinical features individually, as well as through their multimodal combination. Under the patient-wise setting, predictions are obtained by aggregating multiple fundus image representations with corresponding clinical information for each patient. Deep visual features are extracted using a Vision Transformer (ViT) architecture and classified using classical machine-learning models, with a stacking-based ensemble of the three best-performing classifiers employed to optimize performance. Experiments conducted on the publicly available PAPILA dataset demonstrate strong diagnostic performance, achieving 97.47% accuracy and a 97.50% F1-score for sample-wise multimodal classification, and 98.97% accuracy and F1-score for subject-wise detection. The proposed framework is further deployed as an end-to-end web-based platform to support automated glaucoma screening and clinical decision support.

</details>


### [42] [Property-Constrained 3D Porous Media Reconstruction from 2D Images via Conditional Generative Adversarial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02693)
*Ali Sadeghkhani,Brandon Bennett,Arash Rabbani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于条件生成对抗网络（cGAN）的框架，使用2D薄片图像生成可控孔隙度的3D多孔介质，无需3D训练数据，实现了高精度的孔隙度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决3D多孔介质重建中需要昂贵3D训练数据的问题，同时保持对岩石物理属性的控制，特别是在石油地质和储层表征领域。

Method: 开发了基于条件生成对抗网络（cGAN）的混合架构，包含3D生成器和2D判别器，采用多轴切片提取技术从2D数据学习3D结构一致性，并使用增强U-Net分割模型提取孔隙度标签。

Result: 成功生成具有真实岩石学特征的3D体积（包含硬石膏包裹体和细晶结构），孔隙度控制精度达到R²=0.93，异质性和均质样品平均绝对误差分别为0.019和0.010。

Conclusion: 该框架能够仅使用2D图像训练有效生成可控孔隙度的3D多孔介质，显著降低了数据获取成本，为储层建模提供了实用工具。

Abstract: This study presents a conditional Generative Adversarial Network (cGAN) framework for generating 3D porous media volumes with controlled porosity, trained exclusively on 2D thin section images. The key innovation lies in combining property-conditioned generation with 2D-to-3D reconstruction, eliminating the need for expensive 3D training data while maintaining control over petrophysical properties. The framework employs a hybrid architecture with a 3D generator and 2D discriminator, where multi-axis slice extraction enables learning 3D-consistent structures from 2D training data. Porosity labels are extracted using an Enhanced U-Net segmentation model. The methodology was demonstrated on two carbonate samples with different lithologies: dolomite-anhydrite and pure dolomite. Results show that the framework successfully generates realistic 3D volumes capturing lithological features such as anhydrite inclusions and fine crystalline textures. Porosity control achieved an $R^2$ of 0.93, with mean absolute errors of 0.019 and 0.010 for the heterogeneous and homogeneous samples, respectively.

</details>


### [43] [Aircraft Detection in Satellite Imagery using Deep Learning Object Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02699)
*Mujahir Hussain Abbasi,S. Beghin,A. T. R. Krishna Priya,P. Jamuna,G. Anandhakumari,S. Jaanaa Rubavathy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种结合图像增强和深度学习的卫星图像目标检测框架，通过Gabor滤波器降噪和归一化预处理，使用基于YOLOv11的模型进行目标检测，在飞机检测任务中取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星图像目标检测具有重要的实际应用价值（如监控、空中交通监测和遥感分析），但面临噪声大、图像质量波动、背景复杂等固有挑战。现有方法在处理这些问题时存在精度不足的问题，需要更准确的检测框架。

Method: 提出了一个两阶段框架：第一阶段使用Gabor滤波器处理输入图像，突出重要特征并降低噪声；第二阶段应用归一化技术确保数据均匀分布，为模型训练提供良好数据基础；第三阶段采用基于YOLOv11的深度学习模型快速学习和识别目标特征。

Result: 提出的方法在飞机检测任务中取得了：mAP为95%，精确度为97%，召回率为85%，F1得分为91%。这些结果表明框架在卫星图像中准确识别飞机的能力优越，适合实时应用。

Conclusion: 该框架通过结合图像增强和深度学习技术，有效解决了卫星图像目标检测中的噪声和复杂背景问题，证明了其在飞机检测方面的优越性能，适合实际应用场景如监控、空中交通监测和遥感分析。

Abstract: The object detection in satellite imagery has garnered considerable attention due to its extensive real-world applications and the inherent challenges it presents, including noise, fluctuating image quality, and intricate backgrounds. This paper proposed a framework for object detection that combines image enhancement and Deep Learning (DL) to make detection more accurate. First, a Gabor filter is used to process the input image to bring out important features and reduce noise. Then, normalization is applied to make sure that the data is evenly distributed so that the model works properly. After that, a model based on YOLOv11 is used to quickly learn and find object features. The proposed method achieves a mAP of 95%, precision of 97%, recall of 85%, and F1-score of 91%, which demonstrates the superior aircraft detection performance. These results show the framework accurately identify aircraft in satellite imagery and is suitable for real-time applications such as surveillance, air traffic monitoring and remote sensing analysis.

</details>


### [44] [VLRC: Vision-Language Reprojection Consistency as a scalable signal for better feed-forward 3D pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02707)
*Marwane Hariat,David Filliat,Antoine Manzanera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Vision-Language Reprojection Consistency (VLRC)方法，通过冻结的视觉-语言表征提供语义多视角监督，无需额外3D标注即可改进3D重建质量并支持开放词汇3D场景理解


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3D模型训练依赖昂贵的几何监督或自监督光度目标，这两种方法都只能提供不完全的学习信号。需要一种可扩展的辅助目标来利用视觉-语言表征作为语义监督。

Method: VLRC从预测的3D重建中重新投影密集的视觉-语言特征到不同视角，并强制对应图像位置间的特征一致性。该方法无需额外3D标注，可与自监督单目重建和监督预训练的前馈3D模型无缝集成。

Result: 在室内和室外基准测试中，VLRC一致地提高了3D重建准确性，并实现了零样本开放词汇3D语义分割。该方法改进了深度和相机估计，实现了更连贯的多视角语义融合。

Conclusion: VLRC通过利用冻结的视觉-语言表征作为语义多视角监督，提供了一种有效的辅助训练目标，能够在不需要额外标注的情况下改善3D重建质量并支持开放词汇3D场景理解。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D models are commonly trained using either expensive geometric supervision or self-supervised photometric objectives, both of which provide incomplete learning signals. We introduce Vision-Language Reprojection Consistency (VLRC), a scalable auxiliary objective that exploits frozen vision-language representations as semantic multi-view supervision. Given a predicted 3D reconstruction, VLRC reprojects dense vision-language features across views and enforces feature consistency between corresponding image locations, requiring no additional 3D annotations. The objective integrates seamlessly with both self-supervised monocular reconstruction and supervised-pretrained feed-forward 3D models during unlabeled adaptation. By aligning geometry with language-grounded features, VLRC not only improves depth and camera estimation but also enables more coherent multi-view semantic fusion for open-vocabulary 3D scene understanding. Experiments on indoor and outdoor benchmarks demonstrate consistent gains in 3D reconstruction accuracy and zero-shot open-vocabulary 3D semantic segmentation.

</details>


### [45] [When Does Resolution Help a Frozen Backbone? Global Attention at Resolution Predicts Scalable Adaptation for Camouflaged and Marine Animal Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02708)
*Tyler Rust,Chandra Kambhamettu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过研究骨干网络对低秩适配器性能的影响，发现各向同性的视觉Transformer在细粒度分割中表现更佳，提出的SALT方法在多个伪装和显著目标检测数据集上达到新state-of-the-art。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前冻结视觉基础模型在细粒度分割中的性能高度依赖于骨干网络的选择，特别是全局注意力机制与高分辨率token集的关系。研究者希望探索哪些骨干网络特性能够使低秩适配器更好地利用分辨率提升性能。

Method: 研究者进行了六个骨干网络的对照研究，分析不同架构（各向同性ViT与分层骨干）的影响。提出了SALT方法（Side-stem、Attention-gated U-Net、Low-rank Tuning），在固定的框架下仅使用RGB图像进行单次推理。

Result: 各向同性ViT通过全局注意力处理高分辨率token集，随着分辨率提高性能持续改善；分层骨干因早期注意力局限在局部窗口并通过池化降低分辨率，性能在较低分辨率就达到平台期。SALT方法在四个数据匹配的伪装数据集上获得最佳S-measure，在所有海洋和显著目标数据集上领先，在MAS3K基准测试中mIoU达到0.878的新state-of-the-art。

Conclusion: 骨干网络的全局注意力机制是低秩适配器将分辨率转化为精度的关键因素，各向同性ViT在这方面具有优势。这种效应是低秩适配特有的，SALT方法验证了这一发现并在多个细粒度分割任务中取得了最佳性能。

Abstract: Adapting frozen vision foundation models to fine-grained segmentation now largely depends on backbone selection. Whether the backbone applies global attention to a high-resolution token set predicts whether a low-rank adapter turns resolution into accuracy. Isotropic ViTs attend globally over the full grid and keep improving with resolution; hierarchical backbones confine early attention to local windows and pool the grid before their global stages, plateauing at lower resolutions. A controlled six-backbone study establishes the pattern, and editing the backbone points to the cause: pooling keeps the benefit, removing global attention does not. The effect is specific to low-rank adaptation. Under one fixed pipeline, SALT (Side-stem, Attention-gated U-Net, Low-rank Tuning), one RGB-only pass on a strong isotropic backbone wins the best S-measure on the four data-matched camouflaged sets, and leads every marine and salient set. It reaches a new state of the art on both marine-animal benchmarks (MAS3K mIoU 0.878).

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [46] [Improving LLMs via Validator-to-Generator Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02668)
*Juan Diego Rodriguez,Jocelyn Zhang,Katrin Erk,Greg Durrett*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种频率校正的生成器-验证器一致性方法（FCPA），通过校正utterance频率来解决LLMs中生成器-验证器不一致的问题，显著提升了模型的一致性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型存在不一致性问题：提示的变化或无关信息的加入会导致模型输出意外变化。生成器-验证器（G-V）差距是这一现象的表现之一，即LLMs生成回应后，如果重新要求验证这些回应，模型会认为它们是无效的。

Method: 引入了一种新的G-V一致性形式化方法，包含对utterance频率的原则性校正。通过自然的多答案问题回答的理性代理模型，推导出验证器与频率校正生成器评分的一致性。提出了FCPA训练目标，实现对真实LLMs的频率校正G-V一致性训练。

Result: 实验结果表明，使用FCPA训练显著改善了G-V一致性和生成器性能，相比先前方法在IFEval和HumanEval上的Pearson相关性提升了高达+27个百分点，同时在所有评估任务中保持了验证器质量。

Conclusion: 频率校正的生成器-验证器一致性是解决LLMs不一致性问题的有效方法，FCPA框架能够显著提升模型的一致性表现，同时保持验证能力。

Abstract: Large language models are inconsistent: varying prompts or including unrelated information can lead to unexpected changes in model outputs. The generator-validator (G-V) gap is one manifestation of this phenomenon, where LLMs generate responses that they then deem as invalid if re-queried to validate them. In this work, we introduce a new formulation of G-V consistency that involves a principled correction for utterance frequency. Specifically, generators often assign low likelihood to valid strings simply because those strings are a priori unlikely, which makes naive notions of G-V consistency unworkable. We show that under a natural model of rational agents answering questions with multiple answers, consistency of the validator with a frequency-corrected generator score emerges naturally. Our method, \emph{\FCPAname} (\FCPA), is a training objective implementing frequency-corrected G-V consistency for real-world LLMs. Our experimental results show that training with \FCPA{} substantially improves both G-V consistency and generator performance over prior methods, with gains of up to $+27$pp in Pearson correlation on IFEval and HumanEval, while preserving validator quality across all evaluated tasks.

</details>


### [47] [Echoes of Unrest: A Multimodal NLP Framework for Early Warning of Fake News and Violence-Driven Mob Activity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02734)
*Md. Maruf Bangabashi,Tahmid Hasan,Golam Mahmud,Md. Mostafijur Rahman,Md. Toufiqur Rahman,Jahanur Biswas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一个多语言多模态NLP框架，用于社交媒体中虚假信息和暴力倾向动态的早期检测，在孟加拉语和英语混合数据集上达到98%的测试准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体的快速发展虽然促进了全球信息交流，但也加速了虚假信息的传播。假新闻、篡改内容和煽动性叙事与社会动荡、政治不稳定和群体暴力密切相关，尤其是在南亚等地，虚假信息通过Facebook和WhatsApp等平台传播速度快于事实核查能力，导致真实世界危害。

Method: 采用多语言多模态NLP框架，融合了XLM-RoBERTa用于多语言文本表示、CLIP用于视觉嵌入，以及多头注意力机制进行多模态融合。框架还整合了辅助特征如讽刺检测和地理空间元数据。创建了一个包含138,256个孟加拉语和英语样本的融合数据集，结合了多个基准数据集。

Result: 在分层30%子集上的实验实现了98%的测试准确率，具有较高的精确率和召回率。结果显示多模态方法在早期虚假信息检测中的有效性，并强调地理空间信号对预测现实世界冲突升级的附加价值。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了整合多语言、多模态特征和辅助信号（特别是地理空间元数据）的框架在早期检测虚假信息和暴力倾向内容方面的有效性，为应对社交媒体信息威胁提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Rapid growth in social media has transformed global communication by enabling fast information exchange, but it has also accelerated the spread of misinformation. Fake news, manipulated content, and provocative narratives are increasingly linked to social unrest, political instability, and mob violence. Incidents in South Asia and elsewhere demonstrate how false information disseminated via platforms such as Facebook and WhatsApp can trigger real-world harm, often spreading faster than fact-checking efforts can respond. To address this challenge, this chapter presents a multilingual, multimodal Natural Language Processing (NLP) framework for early detection of misinformation and violence-prone dynamics. A fused dataset of 138,256 Bangla and English samples was created by combining multiple benchmark datasets. The framework integrates XLM-RoBERTa for multilingual text representation, CLIP for visual embedding, and a multi-head attention mechanism for multimodal fusion, enhanced with auxiliary features such as sarcasm and geospatial metadata. Experiments on a stratified 30% subset achieved 98% test accuracy with strong precision and recall. The outcomes show the efficacy of multimodal approaches in early misinformation detection and highlight the added value of geospatial signals for anticipating real-world escalation.

</details>


### [48] [Reinforcement Learning for Data-Efficient Code-Switched ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02757)
*Ziwei Ye,Peter Vickers*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于强化学习与可验证奖励的实用方法，用于音频语言模型在代码切换ASR中的高效数据适应，使用10%的数据达到全量数据监督微调的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频语言模型可以处理代码切换语音，但其解码在代码切换方面未优化，经常在语言边界处失败。需要更有效的适应方法来解决这个问题。

Method: 提出RLVR（强化学习与可验证奖励）方法：使用群体相对策略优化，结合错误率奖励和脚本保真度奖励，采用两阶段草稿-精炼过程。以Qwen2-Audio为测试平台，在10种语言对上仅使用TTS生成的代码切换语音进行训练。

Result: 使用10%数据的RLVR方法匹配了全量数据的LoRA监督微调性能，在类型学距离较远的语言对上增益最大。错误率奖励消除翻译错误，脚本保真度奖励单独减少脚本污染而不降低性能。这些增益零样本转移到人工录制的代码切换语料库。

Conclusion: 提出的RLVR方法提供了一种数据高效的方式适应音频语言模型到代码切换ASR，通过可验证的奖励机制有效解决了语言边界和脚本污染问题，且具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Audio-language models can be prompted for code-switched speech, but their decoding is not optimized for code-switching and often fails at language boundaries. We propose a practical reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards recipe for data-efficient adaptation of audio-language models to code-switched ASR using group relative policy optimization, combining an error rate reward with a script fidelity reward that penalizes wrong writing systems and a two-pass draft-and-refinement procedure. Using Qwen2-Audio as a reproducible testbed across 10 language pairs, training on only TTS code-switched speech, we show that RLVR with 10% of the data matches LoRA supervised fine-tuning trained on the full dataset, with the largest gains on typologically distant pairs. The error rate reward eliminates translation errors while the script fidelity reward separately reduces script contamination without degradation. These gains transfer zero-shot to a human-recorded code-switching corpus.

</details>


### [49] [LuxSQA: Ask Me in Luxembourgish with TTS-Augmented Spoken Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02763)
*Nina Hosseini-Kivanani,Marco Matassoni,Alessio Brutti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 使用多源TTS生成的合成语音训练数据可以有效地为低资源卢森堡语口语问答任务创建训练集，无需大规模人工录音语料库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前口语问答研究主要集中在高资源语言和精心录制的语音上，这限制了语音-LLM方法在低资源语言（如卢森堡语）中的应用。研究者希望探索能否使用文本到语音技术为卢森堡语口语问答提供任务特定的训练数据，而不需要大量人工录制的问答语料库。

Method: 1. 从现有的基于文本的问答资源开始，将问题翻译成卢森堡语；
2. 使用多种TTS系统合成口语问题（包括MMS-TTS、Qwen3-TTS和OmniVoice变体）；
3. 将合成语音问题与文本答案配对；
4. 训练参数高效的SLAM风格架构：连接冻结的Whisper编码器到冻结的多语言LLM后端，通过学习的投影器和LoRA适配器；
5. 比较单源语料库（约48k问题）和4TTS多源混合语料库（约230k问题）；
6. 在LLAMA-LB-Test上评估，包含两种真实卢森堡语说话者条件。

Result: 多源和基于语音设计的合成训练配置产生了最强的SQA性能。无参考TTS质量分数不能单调预测下游QA性能，这表明合成语音必须作为任务特定的训练数据进行评估，而不仅仅是作为自然声音的音频。

Conclusion: TTS可以为低资源语言的SQA任务提供有效的合成训练数据，多源TTS方法和语音设计优化比单源方法效果更好。合成语音的评估应该基于其在特定任务中的效用，而不是仅基于其自然度或质量分数。

Abstract: Spoken Question Answering (SQA) remains largely focused on high-resource languages and carefully recorded speech, limiting the reach of speech-LLM methods in low-resource settings. This paper investigates whether text-to-speech (TTS) can provide task-specific training data for Luxembourgish SQA without requiring a large human-recorded QA corpus. Starting from existing text-based QA resources, we translate questions into Luxembourgish, synthesize spoken questions with multiple TTS systems, and pair them with textual answers. We train a parameter-efficient SLAM-style architecture that connects a frozen Whisper encoder to frozen multilingual LLM backends through a learned projector and LoRA adapters. We compare MMS-TTS, Qwen3-TTS, and OmniVoice variants, including single-source corpora of about 48k questions and a 4TTS multi-source mix of approximately 230k questions. Evaluation on LLAMA-LB-Test with two real Luxembourgish speaker conditions shows that multi-source and voice-design-based synthetic training configurations yield the strongest SQA performance. The results also show that no-reference TTS quality scores do not monotonically predict downstream QA performance, indicating that synthetic speech must be evaluated as task-specific training data rather than only as natural-sounding audio.

</details>

Girgis,Alec Go,Alhaad Gokhale,Alex Grills,Yiming Gu,Pramod Gupta,Guru Guruganesh,Raia Hadsell,Hamza Harkous,Jitendra Harlalka,Demis Hassabis,Anja Hauth,Joe Heyward,Arian Hosseini,Chih-Yang Hsia,I-Hung Hsu,Xiaopeng Huang,Yangsibo Huang,Kevin Hui,Adrian Hutter,Te I,Fotis Iliopoulos,Advait Jain,Ganesh Jawahar,Ziwei Ji,Qilin Jin,Melvin Johnson,Kandarp Joshi,Arun Kandoor,Wang-Cheng Kang,Koray Kavukcuoglu,Mehran Kazemi,Kathleen Kenealy,Amr Khalifa,Phoebe Kirk,Suraj Kothawade,Vitaly Kovalev,Neel Kovelamudi,Adam Kraft,Ravin Kumar,Harish Kuppam,Justin Lannin,Chen-Yu Lee,Seungji Lee,Dmitry Lepikhin,Dongdong Li,Qiujia Li,Valentin Liévin,Ethan Lin,Ziqian Lin,Casper Liu,Tianlin Liu,Tianqi Liu,Xin Liu,Mayank Lunayach,Min Ma,Gagan Madan,Andrii Maksai,Eric Malmi,Michal Matuszak,Daniel McDuff,Gaurav Menghani,Daniil Mirylenka,Karolis Misiunas,Vedant Misra,Andreea Mitran,Kareem Mohamed,Maksim Mukha,Eric Noland,James O'Donnell,Kate Olszewska,Bernett Orlando,Wanqiong Pan,Rina Panigrahy,Unnati Parekh,Chunjong Park,Eric Paskie,Liqian Peng,Bryce Petrini,Slav Petrov,Jonas Pfeiffer,Bilal Piot,Martyna Plomecka,Siim Poder,Octavio Ponce,Arijit Pramanik,David Racz,Anish Rajan,Michelle Ramanovich,Anand Rao,Marvin Ritter,Vitor Rodrigues,Evan Rosen,Mikołaj Rybiński,Noveen Sachdeva,Michaël E. Sander,Rohit Sathyanarayana,Sagar Savla,Samuel Schmidgall,Tal Schuster,Benoit Seguin,Andrew Sellergren,Aliaksei Severyn,Izhak Shafran,Dhruv Shah,Yuan Shangguan,Ashish Shenoy,Pradeep Shenoy,Rakesh Shivanna,Pauline Sho,Lucas Spangher,Wojciech Stokowiec,Tim Strother,Yao Su,Yinghao Sun,Mukund Sundararajan,Andrea Tacchetti,Mor Hazan Taege,Pouya Tafti,Chetan Tekur,Rahul Thapa,Madeleine Traverse,Lenart Treven,Tao Tu,Chien Te Tung,Petar Veličković,Malini Pooni Venkat,Sagar Gubbi Venkatesh,Vidya Venkiteswaran,Francesco Visin,Alex Vitvitskyi,Kiran Vodrahalli,Weiyi Wang,Xin Wang,Tris Warkentin,Jan Wassenberg,John Wieting,Lechao Xiao,Hao Xu,Yuhui Xu,Fuzhao Xue,Arun Yadav,Jun Yan,Antoine Yang,Lin Yang,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Ziyu Ying,Jae Hyeon Yoo,Sajjad Zafar,Fred Zhang,Jiageng Zhang,Jianyi Zhang,Xiaofan Zhang,Chao Zhao,David Zhou,Chen Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Gemma 4是Gemma模型家族中的新一代开放权重、原生多模态语言模型，采用密集和混合专家架构，参数范围2.3B-31B，具有统一的编码器自由架构和思维模式，在推理效率、多模态能力和长上下文处理方面显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了推进计算效率和推理能力，开发新一代开放权重的多模态语言模型，改进视觉和音频编码器，提升推理速度、内存效率、计算效率和长上下文处理能力。

Method: 1. 采用密集和混合专家架构，参数范围2.3B-31B；2. 为12B模型提出统一的编码器自由架构，直接处理原始音频和图像补丁；3. 集成思维模式，生成推理轨迹后再响应；4. 通过关键设计选择改进推理速度、内存和计算效率。

Result: Gemma 4在STEM、多模态和长上下文基准测试中实现性能飞跃，在人类评估任务中可与更大规模的前沿开放模型竞争。

Conclusion: Gemma 4代表了Gemma模型家族的重大进步，通过创新的架构设计和功能集成，在计算效率、多模态理解和推理能力方面达到了新的水平，为开放权重多模态语言模型设立了新的性能标准。

Abstract: We introduce Gemma 4, a new generation of open-weight, natively multimodal language models in the Gemma model family. Designed to advance compute efficiency and reasoning, the Gemma 4 model suite features dense and Mixture-of-Experts architectures, ranging from 2.3B to 31B parameters. Alongside improved vision and audio encoders for all model sizes, we propose a unified, encoder-free architecture for our 12B model, which ingests raw audio and image patches. Furthermore, we integrate a thinking mode, enabling Gemma models to generate reasoning traces prior to responding. We improve inference speed, memory, and compute efficiency, as well as long-context abilities through critical design choices. Gemma 4 establishes a leap in performance across STEM, multimodal, and long-context benchmarks, and rivals larger, frontier open models in human-rated tasks.

</details>


### [51] [Seduced by the Narrative: Assessing Rule Adherence in Semi-Open Textual Sandboxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02802)
*Weiying Chen,Junlong Shen,Zhanyuan Guo,Xiaoou Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoC-Seduce是一个基于TRPG机制构建的多智能体对抗基准测试，用于评估LLM在规则遵循方面的脆弱性，特别是针对"修辞注入"攻击，揭示了模型规模和显式推理机制并不能可靠地增强裁决鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地作为自主裁决者在半开放文本游戏环境中部署，当用户意图与系统规则冲突时，模型的规则遵循鲁棒性变得至关重要。然而，这些模型被训练成"乐于助人"和"顺从"的特性，使其容易受到"修辞注入"攻击的攻击，即对抗性用户利用叙事框架技术（如伪逻辑推理和权威胁迫）来绕过裁决逻辑。

Method: 研究人员提出了CoC-Seduce，这是一个基于桌面角色扮演游戏(TRPG)机制构建的多智能体对抗基准测试。TRPG是半开放环境的理想实例化，其中规则明确用于裁决，但交互完全使用自然语言。使用三个前沿模型(GPT-5.4, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 3.5 Flash)作为对抗生成器，在4个世界设定和16个技能类别中生成5,376个样本。然后，使用这个语料库对20个目标裁决模型进行基准测试。

Result: 对20个模型的评估显示：1) 模型规模并不能可靠地增强裁决鲁棒性；2) 显式推理机制也不能可靠地增强裁决鲁棒性；3) 伪逻辑成为主要的攻击向量；4) 跨文化设定暴露了所有评估模型系列中存在的系统性知识差距。

Conclusion: 当前的LLM在作为半开放文本环境中的自主裁决者时，面对修辞注入攻击表现出显著的脆弱性。伪逻辑攻击特别有效，而模型规模和推理增强技术未能提供可靠的防御。这表明需要开发更鲁棒的规则遵循机制，特别是在跨文化背景下，以应对用户意图与系统规则冲突时的裁决挑战。

Abstract: As LLMs are increasingly deployed as autonomous adjudicators in semi-open textual game environments, robust rule adherence becomes critical when user intent conflicts with system rules. However, these models are trained to be helpful and compliant, leaving them vulnerable to a class of attacks we term \textit{Rhetorical Injection}, where adversarial users exploit narrative framing techniques such as pseudo-logical reasoning and authoritative coercion to bypass adjudication logic. We present CoC-Seduce, a multi-agent adversarial benchmark built on Tabletop Role-Playing Game (TRPG) mechanics, an ideal instantiation of semi-open environments where rules are explicit for adjudication, yet interaction remains entirely in natural language. Three frontier models, i.e., GPT-5.4, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 3.5 Flash, serve as adversarial generators producing 5,376 samples across 4 world settings and 16 skill categories. We then benchmark 20 target adjudicators against this corpus. Evaluation across 20 models reveals that neither model scale nor explicit reasoning mechanisms reliably confer adjudication robustness, with \textsc{Pseudo-Logic} emerging as the dominant attack vector and cross-cultural settings exposing systematic knowledge gaps across all evaluated families. Project page: https://github.com/answerrtx/CoC-Seduce

</details>


### [52] [Jointly Improving Dialect Identification and ASR in Indian Languages using Multimodal Feature Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02862)
*Saurabh Kumar,Amartyaveer,Prasanta Kumar Ghosh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TL;DR：提出了一种多模态框架，用于联合改进印度语言的自动语音识别（ASR）和方言识别（DID），通过瓶颈编码器、RoBERTa编码器和门控机制融合语音和文本特征，在33种印度方言上取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度语言多为低资源语言且具有显著的方言差异，现有的ASR和DID方法通常单独优化，导致性能权衡问题。需要一种能够联合改进ASR和DID的方法来处理这种挑战。

Method: 提出的多模态框架包含：1) Bottleneck Encoder从Conformer语音表示中提取方言特征；2) RoBERTa编码器处理ASR生成的CTC嵌入；3) 门控机制融合两种特征；4) 注意力编码器精炼表示；5) 将学习到的嵌入与Conformer输出拼接以增强ASR特征。

Result: 在8种印度语言的33种方言上评估：平均DID准确率达到81.63%，平均字符错误率（CER）为4.65%，平均词错误率（WER）为17.73%。这些结果显著优于单独优化的方法。

Conclusion: 提出的联合ASR-DID建模方法在多语言、多方言的印度语言环境中表现出色，通过多模态特征融合有效解决了低资源语言ASR和DID的协同优化问题，为类似的语言处理任务提供了有价值的参考框架。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) and Dialect Identification (DID) are crucial for Indian languages, many of which are low-resource and exhibit significant dialectal differences. Existing methods often optimize ASR or DID individually, resulting in performance trade-offs. In this work, we propose a multimodal framework that jointly improves ASR and DID. Our method employs a Bottleneck Encoder to extract dialectal features from Conformer-based speech representations and a RoBERTa encoder to process ASR-generated CTC embeddings. A gating mechanism merges these features, followed by an attention encoder to refine the representations. The learned embeddings are concatenated with Conformer outputs to enhance ASR features. Evaluated on eight Indian languages with thirty-three dialects, our method achieves an average DID accuracy of 81.63% and average CER and WER of 4.65% and 17.73%, respectively. These results highlight the effectiveness of our method for joint ASR-DID modeling.

</details>


### [53] [PraMem: Practice-derived Experiential Memory for Long-horizon Behavior Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02881)
*Zhuoqun Li,Boxi Cao,Jiawei Chen,Hanshu Zhou,Ruoxi Xu,Guiping Jiang,Ruotong Pan,Tingting Gao,Han Li,Xiangyu Wu,Hongyu Lin,Yaojie Lu,Xianpei Han,Le Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PraMem提出一种新范式，将长历史序列从负担转变为可利用资源，通过预先实践构建经验记忆来辅助长视野行为预测，显著提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在处理长视野行为预测时面临潜在行为模式归纳和模型内在认知偏差的挑战。现有基于上下文压缩范式的内存管理方法未能解决核心问题，仅试图减轻历史序列负担。

Method: 提出PraMem方法，倡导范式转变：将长历史序列从负担重构为可利用资源。方法包括对长历史序列进行预先实践以构建经验记忆，这些经验记忆随后作为辅助输入用于准确的长视野行为预测。

Result: 在多种任务上的广泛实验表明，PraMem优于现有方法。深入的机制分析为经验记忆的构建和演化提供了有价值的见解。

Conclusion: 将长历史序列视为可开发利用资源而非负担的新范式能够有效解决LLMs在长视野行为预测中的核心挑战，PraMem方法通过构建经验记忆显著提升了预测准确性。

Abstract: Long-horizon behavior prediction aims to infer a user's next action based on a lengthy historical sequence, playing a crucial role in artificial intelligence field. The rise of large language models (LLMs) offers a promising direction for sequential behavior prediction, yet LLMs struggle with latent behavioral pattern induction and model-intrinsic cognitive biases when tackling long-horizon behavior prediction. Prior memory management methods follow a context-compression paradigm that attempts to address this task by alleviating the historical sequence burden, yet fail to resolve the core challenges. In this paper, we advocate a paradigm shift that reframes the lengthy historical sequence from a burden into a valuable resource to be exploited, and accordingly propose PraMem, which conducts beforehand practice over the lengthy historical sequence to build an experiential memory, thereby serving as the assisted input for accurate long-horizon behavior prediction. Extensive experiments across diverse tasks demonstrate that PraMem achieves superior performance than prior methods, and more in-depth analyses provide valuable insights into the mechanism and evolution of the experiential memory. Code: https://github.com/icip-cas/PraMem.

</details>


### [54] [Where do LLMs Fall Short in CBT-Guided Affective Reasoning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02885)
*Vaishnavi Sinha,Pooja Guttal,Pranay Deep Reddy Katike,Vishal Sinha,Gerald Ndawula,Lira Yoon,Andrea Kleinsmith,Manas Gaur*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究显示大型语言模型(LLM)虽然在认知行为治疗(CBT)理论上表现优异(准确率高达96%)，但在实际应用中倾向于默认使用验证与反思策略，无法有效应用CBT治疗。研究者开发了一个知识引导框架，通过多重思维链(MCoT)策略选择来改进模型行为，但效果有限(仅1.2-1.3%改进)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽然能够流利且共情地响应，但在CBT对话中倾向于默认使用验证与反思策略，而不管用户的实际需求。即使模型在理论知识上表现出色(如CBT许可考试中达到96%准确率)，也无法有效地应用CBT技术。研究旨在探索这一理论与应用之间的差距。

Method: 研究者开发了一个知识引导框架，将CBT对话视为受控的情感推理过程：1) 将用户叙述分解为Beck认知概念化结构；2) 基于临床SNOMED CT概念并通过自然语言推理进行验证；3) 使用多重思维链(MCoT)策略在验证与反思、苏格拉底式提问和替代视角之间进行选择。引入了协议杠杆力(F)作为行为级度量指标，衡量干预措施使模型偏离其默认响应的程度。

Result: 在三个开源LLM和14个基于RealCBT的案例研究中，通过人类专家评估、情感唤起轨迹和语言同步性进行评价。结果显示：仅通过单一思维链提示引入协议定义无法改变LLM行为，而MCoT在这些定义上的策略选择表现更好。但效果有限，仅约1.2-1.3%的改进，所有模型仍然偏向验证与反思策略。

Conclusion: 仅具备CBT知识不能确保有效应用CBT技术。研究结果为情感计算社区提供了衡量LLM在哪些方面存在不足的测量工具，揭示了当前LLM在临床应用中的局限性。

Abstract: Cognitive Behavioral Therapy (CBT) provides a structured framework for understanding a user's mental state by examining the interaction between cognitive and behavioral factors. However, out-of-the-box LLMs respond fluently and empathetically, yet collapse into validation & reflection, regardless of what the user actually needs. They know theoretical CBT (scoring up to 96% accuracy on licensing exam questions) but fail to apply it effectively. We explore this gap with a knowledge-guided framework that treats CBT dialogue as controlled affective reasoning: user narratives are decomposed into Beck's Cognitive Conceptualization structure, grounded in clinical SNOMED CT concepts validated via Natural Language Inference, and a Multiple Chain-of-Thought (MCoT) strategy selection between Validation & Reflection, Socratic Questioning, or Alternative Perspectives. To measure whether such guidance actually changes behavior, we introduce the Protocol Leverage Force (F), a behavior-level metric that captures how far an intervention shifts a model away from its default response. Across three open-weight LLMs and 14 RealCBT-derived case studies, evaluated with human experts, valence-arousal trajectories, and linguistic entrainment, F shows that simply introducing protocol definitions via single chain-of-thought prompting fails to change LLM behavior, while MCoT on these definitions guides strategy selection better. Still, the effect stays within 1% (approx. 1.2-1.3%), and all models remain biased toward Validation & Reflection. These results show CBT knowledge alone does not ensure effective application, giving the affective-computing community instrumentation to measure where LLMs fall short.

</details>


### [55] [Distill Where the Student Goes: Teacher-Regularized RL for English-Evidence Cross-Lingual RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02966)
*Haotian Zhou,Weiran Huang,Siqi Liu,Xiting Wang,Xin Zhang,Zhihao Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TR-RAG：一种教师正则化强化学习方法，用于改善跨语言检索增强生成中证据使用和语言一致性的问题，通过结合奖励优化和在策略蒸馏来解决语言漂移和证据使用不可靠的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言检索增强生成（RAG）常部署在英语证据机制下，用户用多种语言查询但检索到的证据仍是英语。这导致两个主要问题：1）英语证据引起语言漂移（生成英语或混合语言输出）；2）模型在生成非英语答案时不可靠地使用证据。这些失败源于两个后训练挑战：前缀依赖的错误和序列级奖励导致的噪声信用分配。

Method: 提出TR-RAG（教师正则化RAG），结合奖励优化和在策略蒸馏。使用紧凑的学生模型采样在策略答案，而更强的冻结教师模型仅在这些访问过的前缀上查询，提供前缀级的学生到教师反向KL锚点。还引入了英语证据多语言生成的奖励分解，结合语言一致性、字符3-gram召回率和LLM判断的证据基础正确性得分。

Result: 在三个基准测试（BioASQ-ENKB5、Hotpot-ENKB5和自然多语言的MKQA）和两个骨干模型上，TR-RAG改善了语言遵守和证据基础正确性的复合指标。教师锚点作为安全网：在领域内语言上防止了纯奖励RL可能遭受的大幅语言一致性崩溃（最高约27个百分点）；在领域外语言上，纯奖励RL停滞在基础模型天花板时，TR-RAG仍改善了证据基础；在字符3-gram召回率上，紧凑学生有时超越了其70B教师。

Conclusion: TR-RAG通过教师正则化强化学习方法有效解决了跨语言RAG中的语言漂移和证据使用不可靠问题。该方法在保持语言一致性的同时提高了证据基础正确性，教师锚点机制防止了纯奖励优化可能导致的性能退化，使模型在领域内和领域外语言上都能稳健改进。

Abstract: Cross-lingual retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is often deployed in an English-evidence regime, where users query in diverse languages but retrieved passages remain English. In this setting, generation can fail despite strong base models: English evidence induces language drift (English or code-switching outputs) and models use evidence unreliably when producing non-English answers. We attribute these failures to two post-training challenges: (i) errors are prefix-dependent, so fixed-trajectory supervision suffers from prefix mismatch; and (ii) sequence-level (partly discrete / judge-based) rewards yield noisy credit assignment and high-variance updates. We propose TR-RAG, a teacher-regularized RL recipe that couples reward optimization with on-policy distillation on student-visited prefixes. A compact student samples on-policy answers, while a stronger frozen teacher is queried only on those prefixes and provides a prefix-wise student-to-teacher reverse-KL anchor. We further introduce a reward decomposition for English-evidence multilingual generation, combining language consistency, character 3-gram recall, and an LLM-judge score for evidence-grounded correctness. Across three benchmarks -- BioASQ-ENKB5, Hotpot-ENKB5, and naturally multilingual MKQA -- and two backbones, TR-RAG improves the composite of language adherence and evidence-grounded correctness over strong baselines. Crucially, the teacher anchor acts as a safety net: on in-domain languages it prevents the large language-consistency collapses (up to ~27 percentage points) that reward-only RL can suffer by drifting below even the base model, while on distant out-of-distribution languages -- where reward-only RL stalls at the base model's ceiling -- it still improves evidence grounding; and on character 3-gram recall the compact student sometimes surpasses its 70B teacher.

</details>


### [56] [Hierarchical Sparse Attention Done Right: Toward Infinite Context Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02980)
*Xiang Hu,Xinyu Wei,Hao Gu,Minshen Zhang,Tian Liang,Huayang Li,Lei Zhu,Yan Wang,Sirui Han,Yushi Bai,Kewei Tu,Haitao Mi,Leo Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HiLS Attention是一种分层次地标稀疏注意力机制，通过学习端到端的块选择来实现高效的长上下文处理，在保持与全注意力相当性能的同时，实现了64倍以上的上下文长度外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大语言模型扩展到长上下文受到两个主要限制：1) 二次计算成本高昂；2) 密集注意力的长度外推能力差。现有的分块稀疏注意力方法由于块选择不准确，性能无法达到全注意力水平。

Method: 提出HiLS注意力机制，采用分层注意力分解：每个查询独立地与检索到的块进行注意力计算以提取块特定信息，然后根据块检索分数融合结果。通过将检索分数整合到前向注意力计算中，使检索分数可以直接在语言建模损失下进行端到端优化。

Result: HiLS注意力在领域内上下文长度下达到与全注意力相当甚至更好的性能；能够外推到训练上下文长度64倍以上，保持90%的检索准确率；现有全注意力模型可以通过轻量级继续预训练转换为HiLS注意力，保持领域内性能的同时获得超长上下文外推能力。

Conclusion: HiLS注意力打破了效率与性能的权衡，通过稀疏KV访问和计算，实现了比全注意力对应模型更高效且在一般长上下文任务上更有效的大语言模型，为长上下文LLM提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Scaling modern large language models (LLMs) to long contexts is limited by the quadratic computation cost, and poor length extrapolation of dense attention. Chunk-wise sparse attention offers a promising alternative, but all existing methods fall short of full attention because of their inaccurate chunk selection. We propose Hierarchical Landmark Sparse (HiLS) Attention, a chunk-wise sparse attention mechanism that learns chunk selection end-to-end under the language-modeling (LM) loss. HiLS factorizes attention hierarchically: each query performs attention independently with each retrieved chunk to extract chunk-specific information, and the resulting outputs are fused according to chunk retrieval scores. By incorporating retrieval scores into the forward attention computation, HiLS optimizes them directly with the LM loss, enabling end-to-end retrieval learning and native sparse training. Experimental results show that HiLS-Attention achieves performance comparable to, and in some cases better than, full attention at in-domain context lengths. Meanwhile, HiLS-Attention extrapolates more than $64\times$ the training context length with 90% retrieval accuracy, far beyond full attention. Moreover, existing full-attention models can be converted to HiLS-Attention with lightweight continued pretraining, preserving in-domain performance while acquiring ultra-long-context extrapolation. Together with its sparse KV access and computation, HiLS-Attention breaks the usual efficiency-performance trade-off, enabling long-context LLMs that are both more efficient and more effective on general long-context tasks than their full-attention counterparts.

</details>


### [57] [psytechlab at CLPsych 2026: Utilising Natural Language Processing methods and Large Language Models for Social Media Text Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03003)
*Igor Buyanov,Nafisa Valieva,Ekaterina Mazurina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 使用多种NLP方法（LSTM、BERT、LLM）分析社交媒体帖子中的心理健康状态和幸福感，在CLPsych Shared Task 2026中取得良好表现


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体帖子是分析心理健康状态和用户幸福感的宝贵数据源，需要开发自动分析工具来改进心理健康支持系统

Method: 采用多种自然语言处理方法，包括长短时记忆网络(LSTM)、基于BERT的模型和大语言模型(LLM)，用于自我状态和幸福感分析及摘要生成

Result: 在CLPsych Shared Task 2026的摘要任务中获得了一致性和矛盾性评分方面的顶级表现，其他任务取得中等水平结果

Conclusion: 通过开发和测试这类心理健康状态评估系统，有助于改进心理健康支持系统，相关代码已开源供社区使用

Abstract: Social media posts are a rich and valuable source of data for analyzing mental health states and users' well-being using automated analysis tools. In this work, we demonstrate how we used a range of Natural Language Processing (NLP) methods, including Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), BERT-based models, and Large Language Models (LLMs), for self-state and well-being analysis and summarization during the CLPsych Shared Task 2026. Our approach achieved one of the top Consistency and Contradiction scores for the summarization task and also middle-level results for the other tasks. By testing and developing such mental health-state estimation systems, we contributed to improving mental health support systems. We make our code available https://github.com/psytechlab/CLPsych2026/.

</details>


### [58] [Alignment-Guided Largest Table Overlap Size Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03049)
*Ge Lee,Shixun Huang,Zhifeng Bao,Shazia Sadiq,Yanchang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ALORE是一个用于快速估计表格间最大重叠区域大小的新方法，它通过显式建模行列结构、引入对齐信号和增强领域鲁棒性，在效率和准确性上都显著超越了现有最佳方法Armadillo。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的估计器Armadillo通过独立嵌入表格并利用嵌入相似度来近似重叠比例，但在异构表格仓库中存在三个主要挑战：(C1) 重叠依赖行列结构，现有编码间接推断这种结构；(C2) 独立编码没有显式的跨表对齐信号通道，导致预测偏向全局相似性；(C3) 朴素的值编码容易过拟合语料库特定分布，导致跨领域性能下降。

Method: ALORE基于三个核心原则设计：(P1) 显式表示行列结构，使用双视图行列超图编码器；(P2) 在训练期间暴露跨表对齐信号而不进行昂贵的对齐搜索，采用对齐引导的目标和低成本交互信号；(P3) 减少对语料库特定值分布的敏感性，采用领域鲁棒的值映射方法。

Result: 在涵盖多种领域和规模的数据集上进行实验，包括一个超出先前基准的大规模真实世界语料库。ALORE整体上优于现有最佳方法：MAE总体降低高达55%，零样本迁移中降低高达69%，同时实现高达89倍的加速。进一步验证了其在按表查询检索中的有效性。

Conclusion: ALORE提出了一种可扩展且领域鲁棒的重叠比例估计器，通过显式建模表格结构、引入对齐信号和增强领域泛化能力，解决了现有方法在异构表格仓库中的关键限制，在准确性、效率和跨领域性能方面都取得了显著改进。

Abstract: Fast estimation of the size of the largest overlap between tables enables blocking and query-by-table retrieval in large table repositories. The first and the state-of-the-art estimator Armadillo improves efficiency by embedding each table independently and approximating overlap ratio via embedding similarity. However, accurate estimation in heterogeneous repositories remains limited by three challenges: (C1) overlap depends on row-column structure, i.e., each matched cell must preserve both its row and column membership under a joint alignment of the two tables, but existing encodings leave this structure to be inferred indirectly; (C2) independent encoding provides no explicit channel for inter-table alignment signals, biasing prediction toward global similarity; (C3) naive value encodings overfit to corpus-specific distributions, causing cross-domain degradation. Hence, we propose ALORE, a scalable and domain-robust overlap ratio estimator built on three principles: (P1) explicitly represent row-column structure; (P2) expose inter-table alignment signals during training without expensive alignment search; (P3) reduce sensitivity to corpus-specific value distributions. ALORE instantiates these principles with a Two-View Row-Column Hypergraph encoder, alignment-guided objectives with inexpensive interaction signals, and a domain-robust value mapping. Experiments on multiple datasets spanning diverse domains and scales, including a large real-world corpus beyond prior benchmarks, show that ALORE outperforms the state of the art. ALORE reduces MAE by up to 55% overall and 69% in zero-shot transfer, while achieving up to 89x speedup. We further validate its effectiveness for query-by-table retrieval.

</details>


### [59] [Don't Wait to Reply: Towards Responsive yet Thoughtful Dialogue through Proactive Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03093)
*Ante Wang,Jiaqi Fu,Xuanyi Chen,Ruotian Ma,Zhaopeng Tu,Weizhi Ma,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Proactive Thinking框架，让大语言模型在对话间歇期预计算可能的回复元素，而不是被动等待下一个输入，以提高实时对话效率和流畅性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的思维过程是反应式的，仅在收到用户回复后才被动触发推理，这必然引入延迟并损害对话流畅性。与人类对话形成鲜明对比：人类会在自然停顿期间主动预测和规划未来内容以确保无缝交互。

Method: 1. 提出Proactive Thinking框架，使模型能够在对话停机时间预计算潜在回复元素；2. 引入无需训练的基础方法，通过预测未来状态进行前瞻性思考；3. 通过推测性连续思考平衡效率与质量；4. 将三个不同复杂度的基准测试适配到时序感知环境中以模拟实时对话流。

Result: 研究表明，主动思维有效提高了交互效率而不影响性能。在模拟实时对话环境的三个基准测试中展示了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 这项工作倡导向更智能、更预测性和实时的对话AI进行根本性转变，通过预计算减少延迟并提高对话流畅性，为实时交互系统开辟新方向。

Abstract: Thinking has emerged as a critical capability for Large Language Models (LLMs) tackling complex tasks. However, its reactive nature, where reasoning is passively triggered only upon receiving a user response, inevitably introduces latency that compromises conversational fluidity. This stands in sharp contrast to human dialogue, where speakers proactively anticipate and plan future content during natural pauses to ensure seamless interaction. To bridge this gap, we propose Proactive Thinking, a framework that empowers models to pre-compute potential response elements during conversational downtime instead of waiting idly for the next input. We then introduce a training-free baseline that can think ahead by anticipating future states, balancing efficiency and quality through speculative continual thinking. To evaluate this approach in practice, we adapt three benchmarks of varying complexity into time-aware environments that simulate real-time conversational flow. We demonstrate that proactive thinking effectively improves interaction efficiency without compromising performance. Ultimately, this work advocates for a fundamental shift toward more intelligent, anticipatory, and real-time conversational AI.

</details>


### [60] [Conditional Diffusion Guided Knowledge Transfer for Multi-Domain Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03154)
*Jiawei Sheng,Taoyu Su,Xixun Lin,Xiaodong Li,Tingwen Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DMKGC：一种基于条件扩散模型的多领域知识图谱补全框架，通过生成领域通用实体嵌入来提升跨领域知识转移效果，特别在低资源场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MKGC方法通过强制等效实体的一致性约束来转移知识，但会抑制实体的领域特定上下文信息，损害所有KG领域的实体表示信息，特别是在低资源数据场景下性能改进受阻。

Method: 提出生成式MKGC范式DMKGC：1）将每个KG视为实体完整信息的部分视图；2）初始化领域无关实体嵌入作为先验嵌入；3）在各KG内编码这些嵌入；4）融合支持KG中的等效实体作为条件扩散生成指导；5）利用先验实体嵌入作为代理生成目标，确保条件生成不偏向任何特定KG；6）同时训练生成嵌入在不同KG间的预测性，保留领域特定信息。

Result: 在3个基准测试的14个KG上实验表明，在尾部实体预测方面平均MRR比最先进方法提升4.3%，在低资源数据设置下保持持续增益。

Conclusion: DMKGC通过条件扩散生成范式有效解决了传统MKGC方法中知识转移时抑制领域特定信息的问题，实现了更优的跨领域知识图谱补全性能，特别是在资源受限场景下。

Abstract: Multi-domain knowledge graph completion (MKGC) aims to improve missing triple prediction in a target KG by transferring knowledge from other support KGs. Existing methods typically enforce consistency constraints on equivalent entities across KGs to transfer knowledge, which risks suppressing domain-specific contextual information of entities. This design can also compromise entity representation information from all KG domains, impeding performance improvements, especially in low-resource data scenarios. To address this, we pioneer a generation-based paradigm for MKGC and propose DMKGC, a conditional diffusion-guided knowledge transfer framework. Our key insight is to treat each KG as a partial view of the entity entire information, and generate informative domain-general entity embeddings through diffusion models conditioned on support KGs. Particularly, we first initialize domain-agnostic entity embeddings as prior entity embeddings, and then encode them within individual KGs. Afterward, we fuse equivalent entities from support KGs as the conditional diffusion generation guidance. We leverage the prior entity embeddings as the proxy generation objective, which ensures this conditional generation to be unbiased towards any conditioned KGs. Simultaneously, we also train the generated embeddings to be predictive across KGs, thus preserving domain-specific information. Extensive experiments on 14 KGs in 3 benchmarks demonstrate a 4.3\% average MRR improvement in tail entity prediction over state-of-the-art methods, with sustained gains in low-resource data settings.

</details>


### [61] [The Role of Prompt Language and Translation-Theory-Driven Prompts in Large Language Models: A Case Study on Spanish-Chinese Journalistic Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03160)
*Haohong Lai,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了提示语言和翻译理论驱动的提示设计对GPT-5.2生成西班牙语-中文新闻翻译质量的影响，通过对比自动评估和人工评估发现理论驱动提示在人工评估中表现更佳，但对语言学习者的教学意义仍需进一步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索人工智能翻译中提示工程的有效性，特别关注翻译理论驱动的提示设计是否能够提升新闻翻译的质量。当前AI翻译工具虽广泛应用，但如何通过精心设计的提示来优化翻译输出，尤其是在特定领域如新闻翻译中的表现，尚需系统研究。

Method: 研究方法采用实验设计：使用48种实验条件（4种提示类型×3种提示语言×4篇文章）。翻译任务基于西班牙《国家报》四篇社论构建平行语料库。评估方法结合自动评估（BLEU和BERTScore-F1）和人工评估（基于多维质量指标MQM框架）。还进行了子错误类型分析以识别具体错误模式。

Result: 主要发现：1）自动评估显示基线提示（BASE）表现最佳；2）人工评估中简要导向提示（BRIEF）得分最高（MQM: 8.66 vs. 7.84）；3）这种评估反转可能源于自动评估的单参考局限性；4）翻译理论驱动提示能选择性减少"尴尬风格"错误，但"不地道风格"错误普遍存在；5）提示语言对两种评估范式的影响均不显著。

Conclusion: 结论表明，翻译理论驱动的提示设计在专家评估下能够为新闻翻译带来可测量的质量提升，特别是在减少特定类型错误方面。然而，这些发现对语言学习者的教学意义仍是建议性的，需要通过用户研究进一步验证。提示工程应关注评估方法的选择，因为自动评估与人工评估可能得出不同结论。

Abstract: This study examines how prompt language and translation theory-driven prompt design influence the quality of Spanish-Chinese journalistic translations generated by GPT-5.2. A parallel corpus of four editorials from El Pais was translated under 48 experimental conditions (4 prompt types, 3 prompt languages, and 4 articles). Translation quality was assessed using BLEU and BERTScore-F1 for automated evaluation, alongside human evaluation based on the Multidimensional Quality Metrics (MQM) framework. Automated metrics identified the baseline prompt (BASE) as the best-performing condition, whereas human evaluation ranked the brief-oriented prompt (BRIEF) highest (MQM: 8.66 vs. 7.84), a reversal likely attributable to the single-reference constraint inherent in automated measures. Sub-error type analysis revealed that translation theory-driven prompts selectively reduced Awkward style errors, while Unidiomatic style errors persisted across conditions. Prompt language had a negligible impact under both evaluation paradigms. These results indicate that translation theory-driven prompts can yield measurable quality gains under expert evaluation of journalistic translations, although their pedagogical implications for language learners remain suggestive and require validation through user-based studies.

</details>


### [62] [KARMA: Knowledge graph-based Automated Reasoning Materialization and Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03166)
*Jinkyeong Choi,Chaebin Jeong,Donghyeon Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: KARMA方法通过知识图谱路径枚举生成槽位对齐的对比候选项，使用槽位并行对齐（SPA）实现解耦的槽级监督路由，解决模板对比合成中的分辨率失配问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模板基对比合成方法虽然可扩展，但其候选项通常只在少数实体槽位上存在差异，而序列级优化会将监督信号分散到大部分共享的模板上，这形成了分辨率失配问题

Method: KARMA方法包含两个核心组件：1）在领域知识图谱上枚举模式约束的路径，并将其语言化为槽位对齐的对比候选项；2）使用槽位并行对齐（SPA）技术，采用解耦的槽级目标将偏好监督路由到判别性实体槽位，可选使用槽感知掩码注意力作为打包评估实现

Result: 在生物医学、计算机科学和化学基准测试中，KARMA超越了基础LLM和相同数据的SFT基线，并且与序列级和令牌级偏好方法相比表现出竞争力

Conclusion: 通过解决模板基对比合成中的分辨率失配问题，KARMA提供了一种有效的槽位对齐对比学习框架，能够在多个专业领域实现性能提升，为对比学习中的监督信号路由提供了新的技术方案

Abstract: Template-based contrastive synthesis is scalable, but its candidates often differ only in a few entity-slots while sequence-level optimization spreads supervision over mostly shared templates. We formalize this as the Resolution Mismatch Problem and propose KARMA, which enumerates schema-constrained paths over domain knowledge graphs and verbalizes them into slot-aligned contrastive candidates. Slot-Parallel Alignment (SPA) then applies a decoupled slot-level objective to route preference supervision to discriminative entity-slots, with slot-aware masked attention serving as an optional packed-evaluation implementation. Across biomedical, computer-science, and chemistry benchmarks, KARMA outperforms base LLM and same-data SFT baselines, and compares favorably with sequence and token-level preference methods.

</details>


### [63] [S-DiverSe: Spanish Diverse Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03207)
*Fernando López,Fernando Ibañez,Ana Martínez,Iván Alonso,Pablo Gómez,Santosh Kesiraju,Jordi Luque*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: S-DiverSe是一个包含3.2小时西班牙语神经性疾病患者语音的数据集，包含ALS、帕金森和中风患者的语音，用于西班牙语神经性疾病语音的ASR评估和开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管标准语音的自动语音识别（ASR）已取得显著进展，但受神经性疾病影响的语音仍然是一个挑战。缺乏专门针对西班牙语神经性疾病语音的"野外"数据集和基准。

Method: 创建S-DiverSe数据集，包含3.2小时的西班牙语"野外"语音，来自22名患有肌萎缩侧索硬化症（ALS）、帕金森病和中风的说话者。数据集包含444个手动转录的音频片段，配有说话者性别、疾病类型和可懂度等元数据。

Result: 进行了基线ASR评估和初步适应实验，发现启发式文本后处理比微调更能适应领域外的西班牙语神经性疾病语音，强调了专门针对西班牙语"野外"数据集的重要性。

Conclusion: 研究强调了针对神经性疾病影响的西班牙语语音开发专门基准的必要性，并表明在当前阶段，简单的文本后处理比模型微调在适应这类语音方面更有效。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) has advanced remarkably for standard speech, yet speech affected by neurological conditions remains a challenge. We present S-DiverSe (Spanish Diverse Speech), a corpus of 3.2 hours of in-the-wild Spanish speech from 22 speakers with amyotrophic lateral sclerosis, Parkinson's disease, and stroke. The dataset contains 444 manually transcribed audio segments with metadata on speaker sex, disease type, and intelligibility. S-DiverSe is designed to support ASR evaluation and development for neurologically affected Spanish speech. We describe the dataset, analyze its composition, and report baseline ASR results alongside initial adaptation experiments. Our findings reveal that heuristic text post-processing is more robust than fine-tuning for out-of-domain neurological Spanish speech. This underscores the need for dedicated in-the-wild Spanish benchmarks.

</details>


### [64] [TACG: Trajectory-Aware Commit Gating for Diffusion Language Model Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03236)
*Chengcheng Wang,Tingzhang Luo,Wenhao Li,Jianyuan Guo,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Trajectory-Aware Commit Gating (TACG)的训练自由门级解码器，通过轨迹感知信号决定何时提交生成的token，从而提高扩散语言模型的解码效率和效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散语言模型解码器忽略了解码过程中的轨迹信息，仅基于当前快照提交token，这导致将置信度与提交准备度混为一谈：在上下文不完整时出现的短暂top-1峰值可能被锁定，而有持续跨步支持的候选token反而被延迟。

Method: 提出了TACG解码器，包括两个核心组件：1) Temporal Implicit Logits Guidance (TILG)：通过过去logits的指数移动平均作为自我参考，在自然参数空间中对比当前logits；2) History Gate (HG)：在提交前强制执行短期提案持久性。结合有上限的额外提升预算，形成稳定性约束的提交规则，无需辅助网络或额外前向传播。

Result: 在LLaDA、Dream和LLaDA2-Mini模型上，针对代码生成（HumanEval、MBPP）和数学推理（GSM8K、MATH500）基准进行评估。TACG通常能够提高或保持准确性，同时减少去噪步骤并增加每个前向传播的token数量（TPF）。

Conclusion: TACG是一种有效的解码方法，能够利用扩散语言模型的轨迹信息改善解码决策，在不增加计算开销的情况下提升生成质量和效率，为扩散语言模型的解码提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising masked positions, exposing a trajectory of predictive distributions rather than a single instantaneous belief. Most existing decoders ignore this trajectory and commit tokens from the current snapshot alone, conflating confidence with commitment readiness: a transient top-1 peak under incomplete context can be locked in, while candidates with consistent cross-step support are delayed. We propose Trajectory-Aware Commit Gating (TACG), a training-free gate-level decoder that anchors token identities to the base posterior and uses trajectory-aware signals only to decide whether the current proposal is ready to commit. TACG combines Temporal Implicit Logits Guidance (TILG), which keeps an exponential moving average of past logits as a self-reference and contrasts the current logits against this reference in natural-parameter space, with a History Gate (HG) that enforces short-term proposal persistence before commitment. Together with a capped extra-promotion budget, these components yield a stability-constrained commit rule without auxiliary networks or extra forward passes. We evaluate TACG on LLaDA, Dream, and LLaDA2-Mini across code (HumanEval, MBPP) and math (GSM8K, MATH500) benchmarks; it typically improves or preserves accuracy while reducing denoising steps and increasing tokens per forward (TPF). The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Clarence-CV/TACG-DLLM.

</details>


### [65] [From Gentlemen to Frontiermen: Masculine Formations in English-Language Fiction (1771--1930)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03323)
*Rong Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文使用150部英美经典小说，通过无监督主题模型识别了19世纪小说中六种男性气质形态，发现基于继承地位和神圣权威的形态下降，而围绕有偿工作和冒险的形态上升，特别是帝国主义/冒险形态增长最为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 19世纪小说中的男性气质并非单一理想，而是一个相互竞争的脚本领域。研究者旨在探究在这一历史时期中，不同男性权威模型的相对重要性如何变化，以理解男性权威的重新组织过程。

Method: 研究基于txtLAB Novel450语料库中的150部英美经典小说（1771-1930年）。首先使用指代消解提取男性角色为中心的文本窗口，将名称、名词指称和代词分组为角色特定的指称链。然后拟合无监督结构化主题模型，将出版年份和作者性别作为主题流行度的协变量。

Result: 模型识别出六种不同的男性气质形态：贵族-骑士精神、基督教男子气概、绅士风度、乡绅、专业-商业以及帝国主义/冒险形态。在整个语料库中，与继承等级和神圣权威相关的形态下降，而围绕有偿工作和冒险的形态上升。最大的增长出现在帝国主义/冒险男性气质中，特别是边疆-荒野风格。此外，冒险和商业形态在男性作者创作的小说中更为普遍。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了19世纪男性权威从继承和神圣地位向获得性、商业性和扩张性形式的重新分配。该方法在没有种子词汇的情况下识别出的形态与独立学术研究建立的类别一致，为测量19世纪性别权威的重组提供了可复制的方法。

Abstract: Masculinity in nineteenth-century fiction is not a single ideal but a field of competing scripts. Drawing on 150 British and American canonical novels from the txtLAB Novel450 corpus, published between 1771 and 1930, this paper examines the changing relative prominence of competing models of masculine authority. To focus the analysis on masculine characterisation, the study extracts male-character-centred text windows by using coreference resolution to group names, nominal mentions, and pronouns into character-specific reference chains. It then fits an unsupervised structural topic model with publication year and author gender as topic-prevalence covariates. The model identifies six distinct masculine formations: aristocratic-chivalric, Christian manhood, gentlemanly respectability, country squire, professional-commercial, and imperial/adventure. Across the corpus, formations tied to inherited rank and sacred authority decline, while those organised around paid work and adventure rise. The largest increase occurs not in professional-commercial breadwinning but in imperial/adventure masculinity, particularly the frontier-wilderness register. The trajectory points to a reallocation from inherited and sacred status towards achieved, commercial, and expansionary forms of masculine authority. Adventurous and commercial formations are also more prevalent in novels by authors recorded as male. Because these formations emerge without a seeded vocabulary yet align with categories established in independent scholarship, the article offers a reproducible method for measuring the reorganisation of gendered authority across the long nineteenth century.

</details>


### [66] [From Judgments to Issues: Structured Extraction of Legal Reasoning with Citation-Hallucination Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03325)
*Giovanni Piccioli,Alessia Fidelangeli,Piera Santin,Pierpaolo Vivo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个自动化管道，将意大利税务法院判决分解为独立法律问题，并为每个问题提取基于IRAC框架和法律三段论的结构化XML表示，包含幻觉检测和验证机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要处理约33万份意大利税务法院的一审和二审判决，但传统大语言模型在法律引用方面存在不可靠性，且需要以经济高效的方式处理大量文档。

Method: 使用DeepSeek V3作为基础模型，结合专用解析器(Linkoln)进行引用识别，并设计自动幻觉检测过滤器来比较模型生成的引用与解析器识别的引用(使用URN-NIR、ECLI、CELEX标准标识符)。通过50份由税法博士标注的判决进行验证，计算标注者间一致性和LLM与专家间一致性。

Result: 开发了首个意大利税务法院判决的问题级、专家验证的结构化提取管道，具备幻觉控制机制，为下游应用(如问题级检索、引用网络分析、大规模法律推理数据集构建)提供了具体起点。

Conclusion: 这是首个针对意大利税务法院判决的问题级、专家验证的结构化提取管道，具有幻觉控制功能，为实现经济高效的法律文档处理提供了可行的技术方案，并支持多种法律信息学应用。

Abstract: We present an automated pipeline that decomposes Italian tax-court judgments into individual legal issues and extracts, for each issue, a structured XML representation grounded in the IRAC framework and the legal syllogism. The pipeline targets a corpus of approximately $330{,}000$ first- and second-instance decisions of the Italian tax courts and is built around a capable yet cost-efficient general-purpose model (DeepSeek V3), a choice driven by the need to process several hundred thousand documents at a sustainable cost. To address the well-documented unreliability of large language models on legal citations, we couple the extraction step with an automatic hallucination-detection filter that compares the references produced by the model with those identified in the judgment text by a dedicated parser (Linkoln), normalised to standard identifiers (URN-NIR, ECLI, CELEX). We validate the pipeline on $50$ judgments annotated by two PhDs in tax law, computing inter-annotator agreement and LLM-vs-expert agreement on both issue extraction and legal citations, together with a stand-alone evaluation of the hallucination filter. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first issue-level, expert-validated structured extraction pipeline with hallucination control for Italian tax-court decisions, and it provides a concrete starting point for downstream applications such as issue-level retrieval, citation-network analysis, and the construction of large-scale datasets of legal reasoning.

</details>


### [67] [Efficient Decentralized Multi-task Dataset Valuation via Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03346)
*Mohammadsajad Alipour,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DMVM是一个去中心化的多任务数据集估值框架，通过模型合并而非重新训练来量化不同数据集在多个任务上的贡献，实现了高效、可扩展且保护隐私的数据估值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集估值方法主要局限于单任务场景，无法处理多任务优化需求；传统方法（如基于Shapley值或重新训练）计算成本高，不适合去中心化环境；现有方法在隐私约束和缺乏可信中央协调器的情况下适用性差。

Method: 提出DMVM框架：利用任务算术从模型组合直接推断数据集贡献，绕过重新训练和数据共享；通过量化不同数据集训练的模型在参数空间中的组合方式，推断每个数据集在多个任务上的边际效用；引入安全聚合协议支持去中心化部署，保护个体模型参数和私有数据。

Result: 在计算机视觉和自然语言处理任务上进行了全面实验验证；提供了理论误差界，描述了DMVM的近似质量；框架具有可扩展性、计算效率，并能明确对齐多任务泛化行为。

Conclusion: DMVM为去中心化多任务数据集估值提供了一种创新方法，解决了现有方法的计算效率、隐私保护和多任务适应性等关键限制，为公平透明的数据市场奠定了技术基础。

Abstract: Accurate and efficient dataset valuation is essential for enabling fair and transparent data marketplaces, especially when multiple contributors provide data for training multi-task models. Most existing valuation methods, however, are limited to single-task settings, overlooking scenarios where a buyer aims to optimize performance across multiple downstream tasks. Moreover, traditional valuation approaches, such as Shapley-based or retraining-based methods, are computationally expensive and poorly suited for decentralized environments without a trusted central coordinator and with strict privacy constraints. We propose DMVM (Decentralized Multi-task Valuation via Model Merging), a novel framework that bypasses retraining and data sharing by leveraging task arithmetic to infer dataset contributions directly from model combinations. Instead of retraining or sharing raw data, DMVM quantifies how models trained on different datasets combine in parameter space to infer each dataset's marginal utility across multiple tasks. This formulation yields a valuation process that is scalable, computationally efficient, and explicitly aligned with multi-task generalization behavior. To support decentralized deployment, we introduce a secure aggregation protocol that enables collaborative valuation without revealing individual model parameters or private data. We also provide theoretical error bounds characterizing the approximation quality of DMVM and validate our framework through comprehensive experiments on computer vision and natural language processing tasks.

</details>


### [68] [Spectral Signatures of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03377)
*Zhuoying Zhang,Ishan V. Prasad,Yuanzhe Hu,Zihang Liu,Hengrui Luo,Pu Ren,Yaoqing Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 这篇论文提出了一种基于谱形签名的LLM量化与管理方法，使用权重矩阵的经验谱密度形状作为模型的紧凑谱签名，能够有效追踪模型谱系、聚类相似模型和量化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前公开可用的LLM数量快速增长，但缺乏系统管理和量化这些模型的工具。传统任务特定基准测试无法有效应对LLM在架构、规模和训练流程上的巨大差异，需要一种通用的模型级分析方法。

Method: 基于重尾自正则化理论，采用谱形度量方法，使用权重矩阵经验谱密度（ESD）的形状信息作为每个模型的紧凑谱签名。该方法数据无关、计算高效、尺度不变，适用于大规模分析。

Result: 构建了一个包含主要开源LLM家族的大型多样化模型语料库，并系统性地对比了谱形与非谱形度量。实验表明，谱签名能够有效追踪模型谱系、无监督聚类相似模型，并能量化模型性能。

Conclusion: 谱签名提供了跨LLM广泛性能趋势的有意义代理，能够实现大规模模型集合的高效组织、比较和分析，为解决LLM系统性管理和量化挑战提供了有效工具。

Abstract: The rapidly growing repository of publicly available large language models (LLMs) presents significant challenges for systematic management and quantification at scale, such as model lineage tracing, licensing, and evaluation. However, task-specific benchmarks are insufficient for this setting, as LLMs differ widely in architectures, scales, and training procedures. To address this challenge, we adopt spectral shape-based metrics for managing and quantifying LLMs based on Heavy-Tailed Self-Regularization theory. Our approach uses the shape information of the weight empirical spectral density as a compact spectral signature of each model. This signature captures intrinsic properties of pretrained models and remains robust during post-training, making it suitable for model-level analysis. In addition, this metric is data-free, computationally-efficient, and scale-invariant, enabling large-scale analysis in practice. Moreover, we curate a large and diverse model corpus consisting of major open-source LLM families, and use it to systematically benchmark spectral and non-spectral metrics across models and downstream tasks. We show that our spectral signature supports the tracking of the model lineage, the unsupervised clustering of similar models, and the quantification of the model performance. Overall, the proposed spectral signature provides a meaningful proxy for broad performance trends across LLMs, enabling efficient organization, comparison, and analysis of large model collections.

</details>


### [69] [The Classics at SemEval-2026 Task 3: Combining Transformer Models and LLM-Generated Annotations for Dimensional Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03414)
*Rafif Alshawi,Amit Raj,Aleksey Kudelya,Alexander Shirnin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了针对SemEval-2026任务3：维度方面级情感分析的方法。研究探索了超越传统分类情感（如正面或负面）的方法，预测细粒度的实值情感"效价"（积极性）和"唤醒度"（强度）分数。团队参与了两个子任务：为给定方面预测这些分数（子任务1）和提取完整的情感细节集合，包括方面、类别、意见及其分数（子任务3）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于超越传统分类情感分析的限制，转向更细粒度的情感维度分析。传统方法通常将情感分类为离散类别（如正面、负面、中性），而实际情感体验在积极性和强度上是连续变化的。研究旨在预测情感的两个核心维度：效价（积极性）和唤醒度（强度），以实现更精准、更符合人类情感连续性的分析。

Method: 对于回归任务（子任务1），采用了基于transformer编码器模型的加权集成方法。针对俄语数据，进一步使用大语言模型（LLM）生成合成情感描述来增强输入。对于提取任务（子任务3），微调了解码器LLM来执行结构化预测，使系统能够同时识别情感元素并估计其数值分数。

Result: 论文提出了一个综合的维度方面级情感分析框架：通过加权集成transformer模型处理回归任务，通过LLM增强俄语输入，以及通过微调解码器LLM实现结构化提取。该方法能够同时处理情感分数预测和情感元素提取，在SemEval-2026任务3中提供了完整的解决方案。

Conclusion: 研究表明，结合transformer模型的加权集成和LLM增强方法，可以有效处理维度情感分析任务。对于回归任务，集成策略提供了稳定的性能；对于提取任务，结构化预测方法能够同时识别多个情感维度元素。该方法为维度情感分析提供了新的技术路线，特别是在多语言环境下结合LLM进行数据增强方面具有创新性。

Abstract: This paper presents an approach to the SemEval-2026 Task 3: Dimensional Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis. We investigate methods for moving beyond traditional categorical sentiment (e.g., positive or negative) to predict fine-grained, real-valued scores for sentiment "valence" (positivity) and "arousal" (intensity). We participate in two subtasks: predicting these scores for given aspects (Subtask 1) and extracting full sets of sentiment details, including aspects, categories, and opinions alongside their scores (Subtask 3). Our approach for the regression task involves a weighted ensemble of transformer-based encoder models. For the Russian language, we further enhance the input by using a large language model (LLM) to generate synthetic sentiment descriptions. For the extraction task, we fine-tune a decoder LLM to perform structured prediction, allowing the system to identify sentiment elements and estimate their numerical scores simultaneously.

</details>


### [70] [CaresAI at SMM4H-HeaRD 2026: Predicting TNM Staging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03466)
*Joseph Itopa Abubakar,Jorge Jarme,Favour Igwezeke,Mary Adewunmi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究旨在使用TCGA病理报告独立预测TNM分期标签，作为SMM4H-HeaRD 2026第六个共享任务。探索了基于TF-IDF特征和临床BERT变体嵌入的经典与深度学习分类方法，评估了多种模型在T、N、M三个多标签分类任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为SMM4H-HeaRD 2026共享任务开发一个能够自动从癌症基因组图谱（TCGA）病理报告中提取和预测TNM分期标签的系统，解决临床文本中自动分期分类的挑战，为临床决策支持提供基础工具。

Method: 1. 问题形式化：将TNM分期预测定义为三个独立的多标签分类任务（T、N、M类别）
2. 特征提取：使用TF-IDF统计特征，以及ClinicalBERT、BioBERT、PubMedBERT等预训练语言模型的嵌入表示
3. 模型架构：比较经典机器学习（逻辑回归、LightGBM）和深度学习模型（前馈神经网络、宽残差网络）
4. 实验设置：在TCGA数据集上进行训练和评估，使用Codabench平台进行测试集评估
